KBSECTION
//REGION Handle updates to the INIT section (or other necessary one-time actions for a new version) without having to re-mod the save

IF
LevelGameplayStarted(_,_)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_CheckForUpdate();

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_CheckForUpdate()
AND
DB_WW_TL_InitVer(_Ver)
AND
_Ver < 8
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_UpdateModVersion(_Ver);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_UpdateModVersion(_Ver)
AND
IntegerSum(_Ver,1,_NewVer)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_InitVer(_Ver);
DB_WW_TL_InitVer(_NewVer);

// v1 -> v2
// Transmogrification names will no longer be dynamically re-determined each time, and will instead be stored upon creation.
// To upgrade, all existing items need to be dynamically determined one last time upon loading

// Short delay to get past the point where the names that get added to DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationDisplayNames will be immediately assigned to items a second time
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_UpdateModVersion(1)
THEN
TimerLaunch("WW_TL_UpdateDelay_1_2",5);

IF
TimerFinished("WW_TL_UpdateDelay_1_2")
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Item,_,_,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationStatsSources(_Source,_Item)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_SetArmorName(_Item,_Source);

IF
TimerFinished("WW_TL_UpdateDelay_1_2")
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Item,_,_,_)
AND
NOT DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationStatsSources(_,_Item)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_SetArmorName(_Item,NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000);

// v2 -> v3
// Adds another column to the end of DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents
// Also creates DB_WW_TL_TooltipStatuses
// Finally, add new tooltip statuses to all existing items after a short delay

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_UpdateModVersion(2)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_UpdateInitFacts(2);
TimerLaunch("WW_TL_UpdateDelay_2_3",5);

IF
TimerFinished("WW_TL_UpdateDelay_2_3")
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Item,_,_,_)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_SetOtherTooltips(_Item);

// v3 -> v4
// Adds support for more inaccessible items (most notably the epilogue camp clothes)

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_UpdateModVersion(3)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_UpdateInitFacts(3);

// v4 -> v5
// Adds conditional AC tooltip statuses

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_UpdateModVersion(4)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_UpdateInitFacts(4);

// v5 -> v6
// Adds damage reduction tooltip to Bonespike Garb

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_UpdateModVersion(5)
THEN
DB_WW_TL_TooltipStatuses("WW_TL_MAG_Barbarian_BoneSpike_Armor","REDUCE_DAMAGE");

// v6 -> v7
// Add new item and clean up the deprecated database that records dye usage

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_UpdateModVersion(6)
THEN
DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(ARM_Boots_Metal_689e8d3e-b79b-417b-8cd2-9d08b2f5e618,ARM_Boots_Metal_WW_TL_8a525698-5669-4643-80d1-cb9c71723bde,"WW_TL_ARM_Boots_Metal",2,0,"Boots",0,1);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_UpdateModVersion(6)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationDyes((ITEM)_Transmogrification,(GUIDSTRING)_DyeTemplate)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationDyes(_Transmogrification,_DyeTemplate);

// v7 -> v8
// Add database for ignoring certain statuses as triggers for AC reevaluation

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_UpdateModVersion(7)
THEN
DB_WW_TL_StatusesIgnoredForAC("WW_TL_REEVALUATE_OVERRIDES");
DB_WW_TL_StatusesIgnoredForAC("WW_TL_REMOVE_UNARMORED_STATUSES");

// Loop back around in case there's more than one version to upgrade
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_UpdateModVersion(_)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_CheckForUpdate();

//END_REGION

//REGION Helpers

//REGION Limit transmogrification display name DB to only store the most recent name for each armor, and reapply names periodically

// Added in v1.0.1
IF
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationDisplayNames((GUIDSTRING)_Item,(STRING)_NewName)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationDisplayNames(_Item,_OldName)
AND
_NewName != _OldName
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationDisplayNames(_Item,_OldName);

// Transmogrification names reset between game sessions because need to use string key for it to be persistent, so dynamic names have to be reapplied periodically
// Changed in v1.0.1
IF
LevelGameplayStarted(_,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationDisplayNames(_Item,_DisplayName)
THEN
SetStoryDisplayName(_Item,_DisplayName);

//END_REGION

//REGION Query that creates temp DB with return value for a translated string, or the string key if there is not a valid translation for it

QRY
QRY_WW_TL_FetchString((STRING)_TranslatedStringKey)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_FetchString(_TranslatedStringKey,_TranslatedStringKey)
THEN
DB_NOOP(1);

QRY
QRY_WW_TL_FetchString((STRING)_,(STRING)_)
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_Val)
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_Val);

QRY
QRY_WW_TL_FetchString((STRING)_,(STRING)_)
THEN
DB_WW_TL_PendingResult(1);

QRY
QRY_WW_TL_FetchString((STRING)_TranslatedStringKey,(STRING)_)
AND
ResolveTranslatedString(_TranslatedStringKey,_TranslatedString)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_PendingResult(1);
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_TranslatedString);

QRY
QRY_WW_TL_FetchString((STRING)_,(STRING)_Fallback)
AND
DB_WW_TL_PendingResult(1)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_PendingResult(1);
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_Fallback);

//END_REGION

//REGION misc helpers

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_NotSupportedMessage((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,(GUIDSTRING)_Target)
AND
GetDisplayName(_Target,_NameKey)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_FetchString(_NameKey,"[Item]")
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_Name)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_FetchString("WW_TL_UnsupportedItemMessage")
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_Warning)
AND
Concatenate(_Name,_Warning,_Message)
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_Warning);
OpenMessageBox((CHARACTER)_Caster,_Message);

// Record dyes that have been applied to items

//GetTemplate(_Dye,_DyeTemplate)
//THEN

// Overwrite old dyes

//DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationDyes(_Item,_Dye)
//AND
//DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationDyes(_Item,_OtherDye)
//AND
//_Dye != _OtherDye
//THEN
//NOT DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationDyes(_Item,_OtherDye);
//PROC_WW_TL_RefundDye(_Item,_OtherDye);

// Only refund the dye if it is a transmogrified armor that was re-dyed

//PROC

//AND
//DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors((GUIDSTRING)_Transmogrification,(INTEGER)_,(GUIDSTRING)_,(STRING)_)
//AND
//GetInventoryOwner((GUIDSTRING)_Transmogrification,_Owner)
//THEN

//THEN

QRY
QRY_WW_TL_UsingStatsFromTemplate((CHARACTER)_Character,(GUIDSTRING)_Template)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_Template,_,_,_,_,_Slotname,_,_)
AND
GetEquippedItem(_Character,_Slotname,_Item)
AND
GetTemplate(_Item,_Template)
THEN
DB_NOOP(1);

QRY
QRY_WW_TL_UsingStatsFromTemplate((CHARACTER)_Character,(GUIDSTRING)_Template)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Item,_,_Template,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrifications((GUIDSTRING)_Character,(ITEM)_Item)
THEN
DB_NOOP(1);

//END_REGION

//REGION query to get the heaviest type of transmogrified armor a character has equipped

// want to guarantee that the query will return true
QRY
QRY_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness((GUIDSTRING)_Character)
THEN
DB_NOOP(1);

QRY
QRY_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness((GUIDSTRING)_Character)
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness_Overall(_Character,(INTEGER)_Heaviness)
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness_Overall(_Character,_Heaviness);

QRY
QRY_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness((GUIDSTRING)_Character)
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness_Chest(_Character,(INTEGER)_Heaviness)
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness_Chest(_Character,_Heaviness);

QRY
QRY_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness((GUIDSTRING)_Character)
AND
DB_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrifications(_Character,_Item)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors((GUIDSTRING)_Item,_Heaviness,_,_)
THEN
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness_Overall(_Character,_Heaviness); // gets rid of duplicate values of _Heaviness

QRY
QRY_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness((GUIDSTRING)_Character)
AND
DB_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrifications(_Character,_Item)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_BreastSlotItem((GUIDSTRING)_Item)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Item,_Heaviness,_,_)
THEN
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness_Chest(_Character,_Heaviness);

QRY
QRY_WW_TL_BreastSlotItem((GUIDSTRING)_Item)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Item,_,_StatsSourceTemplate,_)
AND
_StatsSourceTemplate != NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_StatsSourceTemplate,_,_,_,_,"Breast",_,_)
THEN
DB_NOOP(1);

QRY
QRY_WW_TL_BreastSlotItem((GUIDSTRING)_Item)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Item,_,NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000,_)
AND
GetTemplate(_Item,_AppearanceTemplate)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_,_AppearanceTemplate,_,_,_,"Breast",_,_)
THEN
DB_NOOP(1);

QRY
QRY_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness((GUIDSTRING)_Character)
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness_Overall(_Character,_Heaviness)
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness_Overall(_Character,_OtherHeaviness)
AND
_Heaviness < _OtherHeaviness
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness_Overall(_Character,_Heaviness);

//END_REGION

//REGION query to convert transmog heaviness to category of AC limitation (added in v1.0.6.31)

// Reset query return database
QRY
QRY_WW_TL_ArmorHeavinessToACCategory((INTEGER)_Heaviness)
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_ArmorHeavinessToACCategory(_Category)
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_ArmorHeavinessToACCategory(_Category);

// Check for (camp) clothes
QRY
QRY_WW_TL_ArmorHeavinessToACCategory((INTEGER)_Heaviness)
AND
_Heaviness <= 0
THEN
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_ArmorHeavinessToACCategory(0);

// Check for light armor
QRY
QRY_WW_TL_ArmorHeavinessToACCategory(1)
THEN
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_ArmorHeavinessToACCategory(1);

// Check for medium armor that behaves like light armor
QRY
QRY_WW_TL_ArmorHeavinessToACCategory(5)
THEN
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_ArmorHeavinessToACCategory(1);

// Check for medium armor that actually behaves like medium armor
QRY
QRY_WW_TL_ArmorHeavinessToACCategory((INTEGER)_Heaviness)
AND
_Heaviness >= 2
AND
_Heaviness <= 3
THEN
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_ArmorHeavinessToACCategory(2);

// Check for heavy armor
QRY
QRY_WW_TL_ArmorHeavinessToACCategory(4)
THEN
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_ArmorHeavinessToACCategory(3);

//END_REGION

//REGION query to get character's category of equipped transmogs (added in v1.0.6.31)

// Armor heaviness can get a bit convoluted (e.g. not wearing anything _or_ wearing clothes _or_ light armor
// doesn't limit AC, while medium armor can have heaviness 2, 3, or 5, but heaviness 5 behaves like light
// armor...). This level of technical detail can be obtained with QRY_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness, which also
// returns separate answers for only the chest slot (which affects AC) and overall (which might not affect AC but
// can be relevant for other things).

// However, we usually only care about broad categories and not the specifics of how we get there.
// To make this easier to check, QRY_WW_TL_GetACLimits will return a single number that means:
// 0 = no armor (no transmog equipped or wearing clothes)
// 1 = armor that does not limit AC (light armor or certain types of medium armor)
// 2 = armor that partially limits AC (most medium armor)
// 3 = armor that fully limits AC (heavy armor)

// By default, it only checks the chest slot because this is usually the only one that actually affects AC.
// However, providing a positive integer (usually just 1) as a second argument will check every slot and return
// the largest category found.

// If only given the first argument, use the default value for the second argument
QRY
QRY_WW_TL_GetACLimits((GUIDSTRING)_Character)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_GetACLimits(_Character,0)
THEN
DB_NOOP(1);

// Reset query return database
QRY
QRY_WW_TL_GetACLimits((GUIDSTRING)_Character,(INTEGER)_CheckOverall)
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetACLimits(_LimitCategory)
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetACLimits(_LimitCategory);

// Perform sub-query to get the technical armor heaviness and guarantee that this query evaluates to true
QRY
QRY_WW_TL_GetACLimits((GUIDSTRING)_Character,_)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness(_Character)
THEN
DB_NOOP(1);

// If sub-query did not return anything, then character does not have transmog equipped
QRY
QRY_WW_TL_GetACLimits((GUIDSTRING)_Character,0)
AND
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness_Chest(_Character,_)
THEN
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetACLimits(0);

// ...and the same for overall
QRY
QRY_WW_TL_GetACLimits((GUIDSTRING)_Character,1)
AND
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness_Overall(_Character,_)
THEN
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetACLimits(0);

// Otherwise, perform additional sub-query to convert result to a category
QRY
QRY_WW_TL_GetACLimits((GUIDSTRING)_Character,0)
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness_Chest(_Character,_Heaviness)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_ArmorHeavinessToACCategory(_Heaviness)
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_ArmorHeavinessToACCategory(_Category)
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_ArmorHeavinessToACCategory(_Category);
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetACLimits(_Category);

// ...and the same for overall
QRY
QRY_WW_TL_GetACLimits((GUIDSTRING)_Character,1)
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness_Overall(_Character,_Heaviness)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_ArmorHeavinessToACCategory(_Heaviness)
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_ArmorHeavinessToACCategory(_Category)
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_ArmorHeavinessToACCategory(_Category);
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetACLimits(_Category);

//END_REGION

//END_REGION

//REGION Give transmogrification spells to every player

IF
LevelGameplayStarted(_,_)
AND
NOT DB_WW_TL_Initialized(1)
THEN
DB_WW_TL_Initialized(1);

IF
DB_Players(_Player)
AND
DB_WW_TL_Initialized(1)
AND
HasSpell(_Player,"Target_WW_TL_Transmogrify",0)
THEN
AddSpell(_Player,"Target_WW_TL_Transmogrify",0,1);

//END_REGION

//REGION Spell to take appearance

IF
UsingSpellOnTarget(_Caster,_Target,"Target_WW_TL_Transmogrify_TakeAppearance",_,_,_)
AND
GetTemplate(_Target,_TargetTemplate)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_GetAppearanceFromTemplate(_Caster,_Target,_TargetTemplate);

// Show message if this armor is not supported
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_GetAppearanceFromTemplate((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,(GUIDSTRING)_Target,(GUIDSTRING)_TargetTemplate)
AND
NOT DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_TargetTemplate,_,_,_,_,_,_,_)
AND
NOT DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Target,_,_,_)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_NotSupportedMessage(_Caster,_Target);

// Armor is supported normally
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_GetAppearanceFromTemplate((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,(GUIDSTRING)_Target,(GUIDSTRING)_TargetTemplate)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_TargetTemplate,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate,_,_,_,_Slotname,_,_)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_GetStatsFromEquipped(_Caster,_Slotname,_Target,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate);

// Don't check the big DBs for transmogrified armor unless it's in the small DB that indicates it's DEFINITELY transmogrified
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_GetAppearanceFromTemplate((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,(GUIDSTRING)_Target,(GUIDSTRING)_TargetTemplate)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Target,_,_,_)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_GetAppearanceFromTemplate_Helper(_Caster,_Target,_TargetTemplate);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_GetAppearanceFromTemplate_Helper((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,(GUIDSTRING)_Target,(GUIDSTRING)_TargetTemplate)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_,_TargetTemplate,_,_,_,_Slotname,_,_)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_GetStatsFromEquipped(_Caster,_Slotname,_Target,_TargetTemplate);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_GetAppearanceFromTemplate_Helper((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,(GUIDSTRING)_Target,(GUIDSTRING)_TargetTemplate)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorSlots(_Slotname,_,_TargetTemplate)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_GetStatsFromEquipped(_Caster,_Slotname,_Target,_TargetTemplate);

// If the caster has nothing equipped in this slot then skip ahead and just give the item
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_GetStatsFromEquipped((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,(STRING)_Slotname,(GUIDSTRING)_AppearanceSourceItem,(GUIDSTRING)_AppearanceOnlyTemplate)
AND
NOT GetEquippedItem((CHARACTER)_Caster,_Slotname,_)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_GenerateTransmogrification((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000,NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000,_AppearanceSourceItem,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate);

// If the caster has an item equipped in this slot that is already transmogrified then also skip ahead
// Modified in v1.0.2.4
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_GetStatsFromEquipped((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,(STRING)_Slotname,(GUIDSTRING)_AppearanceSourceItem,(GUIDSTRING)_AppearanceOnlyTemplate)
AND
GetEquippedItem((CHARACTER)_Caster,_Slotname,_EquippedItem)
AND
_EquippedItem != NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_EquippedItem,_,_OriginalTemplate,_)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_GetStatsFromEquipped_Helper(_Caster,_EquippedItem,_OriginalTemplate,_AppearanceSourceItem,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate);

// Added in v1.0.2.4
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_GetStatsFromEquipped_Helper((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,_,(GUIDSTRING)_OriginalTemplate,(GUIDSTRING)_AppearanceSourceItem,(GUIDSTRING)_AppearanceOnlyTemplate)
AND
_OriginalTemplate == NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_GenerateTransmogrification((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000,_OriginalTemplate,_AppearanceSourceItem,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate);

// Added in v1.0.2.4
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_GetStatsFromEquipped_Helper((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,(GUIDSTRING)_EquippedItem,(GUIDSTRING)_OriginalTemplate,(GUIDSTRING)_AppearanceSourceItem,(GUIDSTRING)_AppearanceOnlyTemplate)
AND
_OriginalTemplate != NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationStatsSources(_StatsSourceItem,_EquippedItem)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_GenerateTransmogrification((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,_StatsSourceItem,_OriginalTemplate,_AppearanceSourceItem,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate);

// If the caster has an item equipped in this slot that is not transmogrified, check if it's supported
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_GetStatsFromEquipped((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,(STRING)_Slotname,(GUIDSTRING)_AppearanceSourceItem,(GUIDSTRING)_AppearanceOnlyTemplate)
AND
GetEquippedItem((CHARACTER)_Caster,_Slotname,_Item)
AND
_Item != NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000
AND
NOT DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Item,_,_,_)
AND
GetTemplate(_Item,_EquippedTemplate)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_GetStatsFromEquipped_IfSupported((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,_AppearanceSourceItem,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate,_EquippedTemplate,_Item);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_GetStatsFromEquipped_IfSupported((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,(GUIDSTRING)_,(GUIDSTRING)_,(GUIDSTRING)_EquippedTemplate,(GUIDSTRING)_Item)
AND
NOT DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_EquippedTemplate,_,_,_,_,_,_,_)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_NotSupportedMessage(_Caster,_Item);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_GetStatsFromEquipped_IfSupported((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,(GUIDSTRING)_AppearanceSourceItem,(GUIDSTRING)_AppearanceOnlyTemplate,(GUIDSTRING)_EquippedTemplate,(GUIDSTRING)_Item)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_EquippedTemplate,_,_,_,_,_,_,_)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_GenerateTransmogrification(_Caster,_Item,_EquippedTemplate,_AppearanceSourceItem,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate);

//END_REGION

//REGION Spell to take stats

IF
UsingSpellOnTarget(_Caster,_Target,"Target_WW_TL_Transmogrify_TakeStats",_,_,_)
AND
GetTemplate(_Target,_TargetTemplate)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_GetStatsFromTemplate(_Caster,_Target,_TargetTemplate);

// Show message if target armor is not supported

AND
NOT DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_TargetTemplate,_,_,_,_,_,_,_)
AND
NOT DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Target,_,_,_)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_NotSupportedMessage(_Caster,_Target);

// If the target item is supported for transmogrification
PROC

THEN

// If there's no equipped item, generate an item without an appearance

THEN
PROC_WW_TL_GenerateTransmogrification((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,_Target,_TargetTemplate,NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000,NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000);

// If the equipped item is already transmogrified

AND
GetEquippedItem((CHARACTER)_Caster,_Slotname,_EquippedItem)
AND
_EquippedItem != NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000
AND

AND
GetTemplate(_EquippedItem,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_GenerateTransmogrification((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,_Target,_TargetTemplate,_EquippedItem,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate);

// If the caster has an item equipped in this slot that is not transmogrified, check if it's supported
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_GetAppearanceFromEquipped((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,(GUIDSTRING)_Target,(GUIDSTRING)_TargetTemplate,(STRING)_Slotname)
AND
GetEquippedItem((CHARACTER)_Caster,_Slotname,_EquippedItem)
AND
_EquippedItem != NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000

NOT DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_EquippedItem,_,_,_)

//END_REGION

//REGION Generate and equip new transmogrification item from any stat and appearance

// Don't consider the attributes to match even if they technically both come from the same source if they're camp clothes/shoes because those use different slots
QRY
QRY_WW_TL_MatchingAttributeSources((GUIDSTRING)_StatsSourceTemplate,(GUIDSTRING)_AppearanceOnlyTemplate)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_StatsSourceTemplate,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate,_,_ArmorType,_,_,_,_)
AND
_ArmorType != -1
THEN
DB_NOOP(1);

// Make sure the generation database is properly emptied for this character before starting the process
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_GenerateTransmogrification((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,(GUIDSTRING)_,(GUIDSTRING)_,(GUIDSTRING)_,(GUIDSTRING)_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_GeneratingTransmogrification(_Caster,_Slotname,_StatsSourceItem,_StatsSourceTemplate,_AppearanceSourceItem,_NoAppearanceTemplate,_ArmorType,_AC,_StatsBoost)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_GeneratingTransmogrification(_Caster,_Slotname,_StatsSourceItem,_StatsSourceTemplate,_AppearanceSourceItem,_NoAppearanceTemplate,_ArmorType,_AC,_StatsBoost);

// Handle creating a transmogrification that has the stats and appearance of the same item
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_GenerateTransmogrification((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,(GUIDSTRING)_StatsSourceItem,(GUIDSTRING)_StatsSourceTemplate,(GUIDSTRING)_AppearanceSourceItem,(GUIDSTRING)_AppearanceOnlyTemplate)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_MatchingAttributeSources(_StatsSourceTemplate,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_FetchString("WW_TL_UseOriginalItemMessage")
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_Message)
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_Message);
DB_WW_TL_PausedGeneration(_Caster,_Message,_StatsSourceItem,_StatsSourceTemplate,_AppearanceSourceItem,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate);
OpenMessageBoxYesNo((CHARACTER)_Caster,_Message);

IF
MessageBoxYesNoClosed(_Character,_Message,1)
AND
DB_WW_TL_PausedGeneration(_Character,_Message,_StatsSourceItem,_StatsSourceTemplate,_AppearanceSourceItem,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_StatsSourceTemplate,_,_,_,_,_Slotname,_,_)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_PausedGeneration(_Character,_Message,_StatsSourceItem,_StatsSourceTemplate,_AppearanceSourceItem,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate);
PROC_WW_TL_OverwriteOld(_Character,_Slotname);
Equip(_Character,(ITEM)_StatsSourceItem,1,1);

IF
MessageBoxYesNoClosed(_Caster,_Message,0)
AND
DB_WW_TL_PausedGeneration(_Caster,_Message,_StatsSourceItem,_StatsSourceTemplate,_AppearanceSourceItem,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_StatsSourceTemplate,_,_StatsBoost,_ArmorType,_AC,_Slotname,_,_)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_PausedGeneration(_Caster,_Message,_StatsSourceItem,_StatsSourceTemplate,_AppearanceSourceItem,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate);
DB_WW_TL_GeneratingTransmogrification((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,_Slotname,_StatsSourceItem,_StatsSourceTemplate,_AppearanceSourceItem,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate,_ArmorType,_AC,_StatsBoost);
PROC_WW_TL_Transmogrify(_Caster);

// Handle creating a transmogrification without stats
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_GenerateTransmogrification((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,(GUIDSTRING)_StatsSourceItem,(GUIDSTRING)_StatsSourceTemplate,(GUIDSTRING)_AppearanceSourceItem,(GUIDSTRING)_AppearanceOnlyTemplate)
AND
_StatsSourceTemplate == NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate,_,_,_,_Slotname,_,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorSlots(_Slotname,_DefaultAC,_)
AND
ConcatenateInteger("WW_TL_AC_",_DefaultAC,_ACStat)
THEN
DB_WW_TL_GeneratingTransmogrification(_Caster,_Slotname,NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000,NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000,_AppearanceSourceItem,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate,0,_DefaultAC,_ACStat);

// Handle creating a transmogrification without an appearance
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_GenerateTransmogrification((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,(GUIDSTRING)_StatsSourceItem,(GUIDSTRING)_StatsSourceTemplate,(GUIDSTRING)_AppearanceSourceItem,(GUIDSTRING)_AppearanceOnlyTemplate)
AND
_AppearanceOnlyTemplate == NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_StatsSourceTemplate,_,_StatsBoost,_ArmorType,_AC,_Slotname,_,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorSlots(_Slotname,_,_NoAppearanceTemplate)
THEN
DB_WW_TL_GeneratingTransmogrification(_Caster,_Slotname,_StatsSourceItem,_StatsSourceTemplate,_AppearanceSourceItem,_NoAppearanceTemplate,_ArmorType,_AC,_StatsBoost);

// Handle creating a transmogrification with both stats and appearance
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_GenerateTransmogrification((GUIDSTRING)_Caster,(GUIDSTRING)_StatsSourceItem,(GUIDSTRING)_StatsSourceTemplate,(GUIDSTRING)_AppearanceSourceItem,(GUIDSTRING)_AppearanceOnlyTemplate)
AND
_StatsSourceTemplate != NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000
AND
_AppearanceOnlyTemplate != NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000
AND
NOT QRY_WW_TL_MatchingAttributeSources(_StatsSourceTemplate,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_StatsSourceTemplate,_,_StatsBoost,_ArmorType,_AC,_Slotname,_,_)
THEN
DB_WW_TL_GeneratingTransmogrification(_Caster,_Slotname,_StatsSourceItem,_StatsSourceTemplate,_AppearanceSourceItem,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate,_ArmorType,_AC,_StatsBoost);

IF
CastSpellFailed(_Caster,_,_,_,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_GeneratingTransmogrification(_Caster,_Slotname,_StatsSourceItem,_StatsSourceTemplate,_AppearanceSourceItem,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate,_ArmorType,_AC,_StatsBoost)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_GeneratingTransmogrification(_Caster,_Slotname,_StatsSourceItem,_StatsSourceTemplate,_AppearanceSourceItem,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate,_ArmorType,_AC,_StatsBoost);

IF
CastSpell(_Caster,_,_,_,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_GeneratingTransmogrification(_Caster,_,_,_,_,_,_,_,_)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_Transmogrify(_Caster);

// If the currently equipped item in this slot is a transmogrification that has the same appearance
QRY
QRY_WW_TL_OnlyChangingStats((CHARACTER)_Character,(STRING)_Slotname,(GUIDSTRING)_AppearanceOnlyTemplate)
AND
GetEquippedItem(_Character,_Slotname,_EquippedItem)
AND
GetTemplate((GUIDSTRING)_EquippedItem,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_EquippedItem,_,_,_StatsBoost)
THEN
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_OnlyChangingStats((GUIDSTRING)_Character,_EquippedItem,_StatsBoost);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_Transmogrify((GUIDSTRING)_Character)
AND
DB_WW_TL_GeneratingTransmogrification(_Character,_Slotname,_,_,_,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate,_,_,_)
AND
NOT QRY_WW_TL_OnlyChangingStats((CHARACTER)_Character,_Slotname,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_OverwriteOld((GUIDSTRING)_Character,_Slotname);
TemplateAddTo((ITEMROOT)_AppearanceOnlyTemplate,_Character,1,0);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_Transmogrify((GUIDSTRING)_Character)
AND
DB_WW_TL_GeneratingTransmogrification(_Character,_Slotname,_,_,_,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate,_,_,_)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_OnlyChangingStats((CHARACTER)_Character,_Slotname,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate)
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_OnlyChangingStats((GUIDSTRING)_Character,_Item,_StatsBoost)
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_OnlyChangingStats(_Character,_Item,_StatsBoost);
PROC_WW_TL_RemoveStatsBoost(_Character,_StatsBoost,_Item);
PROC_WW_TL_RemoveTransmogrificationAttributes((GUIDSTRING)_Item);
PROC_WW_TL_SetTransmogrificationAttributes(_Item,(GUIDSTRING)_Character);
DB_WW_TL_Reequip((ITEM)_Item);
Unequip((CHARACTER)_Character,_Item);

IF
Unequipped(_Item,_Character)
AND
DB_WW_TL_Reequip((ITEM)_Item)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_Reequip(_Item);
Equip(_Character,_Item,1,1);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_OverwriteOld((GUIDSTRING)_Character,(STRING)_Slotname)
AND
GetEquippedItem((CHARACTER)_Character,_Slotname,_EquippedItem)
AND
DB_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrifications(_Character,_EquippedItem)
THEN
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationBeingOverwritten(_EquippedItem);

IF
AddedTo(_Item,_Caster,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_GeneratingTransmogrification(_Caster,_,_,_,_AppearanceSourceItem,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate,_,_,_)
AND
GetTemplate(_Item,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_TagArmor(_Item);
PROC_WW_TL_SetTransmogrificationAttributes(_Item,_Caster);
//PROC_WW_TL_GiveMissingDye(_Caster,_AppearanceSourceItem); disabled in v1.0.5.3
Equip((CHARACTER)_Caster,(ITEM)_Item,1,1);

// Should be applied automatically by the armor stats, but it doesn't seem to be???
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_TagArmor((GUIDSTRING)_Item)
AND
IsTagged(_Item,(TAG)WW_TL_TRANSMOGRIFIED_f4a99f6c-34e9-4958-9ce7-3b4d01e61e07,0)
THEN
SetTag(_Item,(TAG)WW_TL_TRANSMOGRIFIED_f4a99f6c-34e9-4958-9ce7-3b4d01e61e07);

AND
DB_WW_TL_GeneratingTransmogrification(_Caster,_Slotname,_StatsSourceItem,_StatsSourceTemplate,_AppearanceSourceItem,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate,_ArmorType,_AC,_StatsBoost)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_GeneratingTransmogrification(_Caster,_Slotname,_StatsSourceItem,_StatsSourceTemplate,_AppearanceSourceItem,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate,_ArmorType,_AC,_StatsBoost);
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Item,_ArmorType,_StatsSourceTemplate,_StatsBoost);
PROC_WW_TL_RecordSourceItem(_StatsSourceItem,_Item);
PROC_WW_TL_SetArmorName(_Item,_StatsSourceItem);
PROC_WW_TL_SetArmorTypeStatus(_Item,_ArmorType);
PROC_WW_TL_SetArmorACStatus(_Item,_AC);
PROC_WW_TL_SetOtherTooltips(_Item);

AND
_StatsSourceItem != NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000
AND
NOT DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationStatsSources(_,_StatsSourceItem)
THEN
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationStatsSources(_StatsSourceItem,_Item);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_RecordSourceItem((GUIDSTRING)_StatsSourceItem,(GUIDSTRING)_Item)
AND
_StatsSourceItem != NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationStatsSources(_OriginalSource,_StatsSourceItem)
THEN
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationStatsSources(_OriginalSource,_Item);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_SetArmorName((GUIDSTRING)_Armor,(GUIDSTRING)_SourceItem)
AND
_SourceItem == NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000
AND
QRY_WW_TL_FetchString("WW_TL_ItemWithoutStatsName")
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_ArmorName)
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_ArmorName);
PROC_WW_TL_SetArmorName_Helper(_Armor,_ArmorName);

// If the source of the armor stats is not itself a transmogrified item (if it is, this would lead to "Name (Transmogrified) (Transmogrified)"
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_SetArmorName((GUIDSTRING)_Armor,(GUIDSTRING)_SourceItem)
AND
_SourceItem != NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000
AND
NOT DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationStatsSources(_,_SourceItem)
AND
GetDisplayName(_SourceItem,_TranslatedStringKey)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_FetchString(_TranslatedStringKey)
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_ArmorName)
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_ArmorName);
PROC_WW_TL_SetArmorName_Helper(_Armor,_ArmorName);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_SetArmorName((GUIDSTRING)_Armor,(GUIDSTRING)_SourceItem)
AND
_SourceItem != NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationStatsSources(_OriginalSourceItem,_SourceItem)
AND
GetDisplayName(_OriginalSourceItem,_TranslatedStringKey)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_FetchString(_TranslatedStringKey)
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_ArmorName)
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_ArmorName);
PROC_WW_TL_SetArmorName_Helper(_Armor,_ArmorName);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_SetArmorName_Helper((GUIDSTRING)_Armor,(STRING)_ArmorName)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_FetchString("WW_TL_TransmogrificationLabel"," (Transmogrified)")
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_Label)
AND
Concatenate(_ArmorName,_Label,_NewName)
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_Label);
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationDisplayNames(_Armor,_NewName); // added in v1.0.1
SetStoryDisplayName(_Armor,_NewName);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_SetArmorTypeStatus((GUIDSTRING)_Armor,(INTEGER)_Type)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorTypes(_Type,_,_DisplayStatus)
THEN
ApplyStatus(_Armor,_DisplayStatus,-1.0);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_SetArmorACStatus((GUIDSTRING)_Armor,(INTEGER)_AC)
AND
_AC > 0
AND
ConcatenateInteger("WW_TL_ARMOR_DISPLAY_AC_",_AC,_Status)
THEN
ApplyStatus(_Armor,_Status,-1.0);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_SetOtherTooltips((GUIDSTRING)_Item)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Item,_,_OriginalTemplateForStats,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_OriginalTemplateForStats,_,_StatsBoost,_,_,_,_,_IncompleteTooltip)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_SetOtherTooltips_Helper(_Item,_StatsBoost);
PROC_WW_TL_SetOtherTooltips_Incomplete(_Item,_IncompleteTooltip);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_SetOtherTooltips_Helper((GUIDSTRING)_Item,(STRING)_StatsBoost)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TooltipStatuses(_StatsBoost,_StatusType)
AND
Concatenate("WW_TL_ARMOR_DISPLAY_",_StatusType,_TooltipStatus)
THEN
ApplyStatus(_Item,_TooltipStatus,-1.0);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_SetOtherTooltips_Incomplete((GUIDSTRING)_Item,1)
THEN
ApplyStatus(_Item,"WW_TL_ARMOR_DISPLAY_OTHER",-1.0);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_RemoveTransmogrificationAttributes((GUIDSTRING)_Item)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Item,_ArmorType,_StatsSourceTemplate,_StatsBoost)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_WipeSourceItem(_Item);
PROC_WW_TL_WipeArmorTypeStatus(_Item,_ArmorType);
PROC_WW_TL_WipeArmorACStatus(_Item,_StatsSourceTemplate);
PROC_WW_TL_WipeOtherTooltips(_Item,_StatsSourceTemplate);
NOT DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Item,_ArmorType,_StatsSourceTemplate,_StatsBoost);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_WipeSourceItem((GUIDSTRING)_Item)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationStatsSources(_Source,_Item)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationStatsSources(_Source,_Item);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_WipeArmorTypeStatus((GUIDSTRING)_Armor,(INTEGER)_Type)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorTypes(_Type,_,_DisplayStatus)
THEN
RemoveStatus(_Armor,_DisplayStatus);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_WipeArmorACStatus((GUIDSTRING)_Armor,(GUIDSTRING)_StatsSourceTemplate)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_StatsSourceTemplate,_,_,_,_AC,_,_,_)
AND
_AC > 0
AND
ConcatenateInteger("WW_TL_ARMOR_DISPLAY_AC_",_AC,_Status)
THEN
RemoveStatus(_Armor,_Status);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_WipeArmorACStatus((GUIDSTRING)_Armor,(GUIDSTRING)_StatsSourceTemplate)
AND
_StatsSourceTemplate == NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000
AND
GetTemplate(_Armor,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_,_AppearanceOnlyTemplate,_,_,_,_Slotname,_,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorSlots(_Slotname,_DefaultAC,_)
AND
_DefaultAC > 0
AND
ConcatenateInteger("WW_TL_ARMOR_DISPLAY_AC_",_DefaultAC,_Status)
THEN
RemoveStatus(_Armor,_Status);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_WipeOtherTooltips((GUIDSTRING)_Item,(GUIDSTRING)_StatsSourceTemplate)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_StatsSourceTemplate,_,_StatsBoost,_,_,_,_,_IncompleteTooltip)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_WipeOtherTooltips_Helper(_Item,_StatsBoost);
PROC_WW_TL_WipeOtherTooltips_Incomplete(_Item,_IncompleteTooltip);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_WipeOtherTooltips_Helper((GUIDSTRING)_Item,(STRING)_StatsBoost)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TooltipStatuses(_StatsBoost,_StatusType)
AND
Concatenate("WW_TL_ARMOR_DISPLAY_",_StatusType,_TooltipStatus)
THEN
RemoveStatus(_Item,_TooltipStatus);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_WipeOtherTooltips_Incomplete((GUIDSTRING)_Item,1)
THEN
RemoveStatus(_Item,"WW_TL_ARMOR_DISPLAY_OTHER");


//END_REGION

//REGION Handle transmogrifications being (un)equipped

// This PROC does not actually do anything in this base mod, but it will be called whenever _Character equips a transmogrification, _Armor.
// Dependencies can use this PROC like the transmog equivalent of the built-in Osiris event Equipped(_Item,_Character)
// Added in v1.0.5.3
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_Event_Equipped((ITEM)_Armor,(CHARACTER)_Character)
THEN
DB_NOOP(1);

// This PROC does not actually do anything in this base mod, but it will be called whenever _Character unequips a transmogrification, _Armor.
// Dependencies can use this PROC like the transmog equivalent of the built-in Osiris event Unequipped(_Item,_Character)
// Added in v1.0.5.3
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_Event_Unequipped((ITEM)_Armor,(CHARACTER)_Character)
THEN
DB_NOOP(1);

IF
Unequipped(_Armor,_Character)
AND
DB_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrifications((GUIDSTRING)_Character,(ITEM)_Armor)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Armor,_,_,_StatsBoost)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrifications(_Character,_Armor);
PROC_WW_TL_RemoveStatsBoost(_Character,_StatsBoost,_Armor);
PROC_WW_TL_ApplyArmorHeaviness(_Character);
PROC_WW_TL_Event_Unequipped(_Armor,(CHARACTER)_Character); // added in v1.0.5.3

IF
Equipped(_Armor,_Character)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrification(_Armor,_Character);

// If IsProficientWith fails altogether, then _SourceItem no longer exists and should be removed from DB
// Added in v1.0.1
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrification((ITEM)_Item,(CHARACTER)_Character)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationStatsSources(_SourceItem,(GUIDSTRING)_Item)
AND
NOT IsProficientWith(_Character,(ITEM)_SourceItem,_)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationStatsSources((GUIDSTRING)_SourceItem,(GUIDSTRING)_Item);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrification((ITEM)_Item,(CHARACTER)_Character)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationStatsSources(_SourceItem,(GUIDSTRING)_Item)
AND
IsProficientWith(_Character,(ITEM)_SourceItem,_Proficiency)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrification((ITEM)_Item,_Character,_Proficiency);

// Handle situation where transmogrification was made without a stats source, which doesn't need a proficiency check
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrification((ITEM)_Item,(CHARACTER)_Character)
AND
//DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors((GUIDSTRING)_Item,_,NULL_00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000,_) // removed in v1.0.1
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors((GUIDSTRING)_Item,_,_,_) // added in v1.0.1
AND
NOT DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationStatsSources(_,_Item) // added in v1.0.1
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrification((ITEM)_Item,_Character,1);

// Prevent characters from equipping armor they aren't proficient with b/c I can't recreate the debuffs it should apply
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrification((ITEM)_Item,(CHARACTER)_Character,0)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_FetchString("WW_TL_NotProficientMessage")
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_Message)
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_Message);
Unequip(_Character,_Item);
OpenMessageBox(_Character,_Message);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrification((ITEM)_Armor,(CHARACTER)_Character,1)
THEN
DB_WW_TL_EquippingTransmogrification(_Character,_Armor);
RealtimeObjectTimerLaunch(_Character,"WW_TL_EquippedArmorEvaluation",10);

// Wait a short time in case another transmogrified item needs to be unequipped first so they don't interfere with each other
IF
ObjectTimerFinished(_Character,"WW_TL_EquippedArmorEvaluation")
AND
DB_WW_TL_EquippingTransmogrification((CHARACTER)_Character,_Armor)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Armor,_,_,_StatsBoost)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_EquippingTransmogrification(_Character,_Armor);
DB_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrifications((GUIDSTRING)_Character,_Armor);
PROC_WW_TL_ApplyArmorHeaviness((CHARACTER)_Character);
PROC_WW_TL_ApplyStatsBoost(_Character,_StatsBoost);
PROC_WW_TL_Event_Equipped(_Armor,_Character); // added in v1.0.5.3

// Check for anyone already wearing other transmogrified armor from the same source that is restricted to one use at a time
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrification((ITEM)_Armor,(CHARACTER)_Character,1)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Armor,_,_StatsTemplate,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_StatsTemplate,_,_,_,_,_,1,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationStatsSources(_StatsSource,_Armor)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationStatsSources(_StatsSource,_OtherArmor)
AND
_Armor != _OtherArmor
AND
DB_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrifications(_OtherCharacter,(ITEM)_OtherArmor)
AND
_Character != _OtherCharacter
AND
QRY_WW_TL_FetchString("WW_TL_ItemAlreadyInUseMessage")
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_Message)
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_Message);
DB_WW_TL_OverlappingArmors(_Message,(CHARACTER)_Character,_Armor,_OtherCharacter,_OtherArmor);
OpenMessageBoxYesNo(_Character,_Message);

// Check for anyone wearing the source of this transmogrified armor's stats that is restricted to one use at a time
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrification((ITEM)_Armor,(CHARACTER)_Character,1)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Armor,_,_StatsTemplate,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_StatsTemplate,_,_,_,_,_,1,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationStatsSources(_StatsSource,_Armor)
AND
IsEquipped((ITEM)_StatsSource,1)
AND
GetInventoryOwner((GUIDSTRING)_StatsSource,_OtherCharacter)
AND
_Character != _OtherCharacter
AND
QRY_WW_TL_FetchString("WW_TL_ItemAlreadyInUseMessage")
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_Message)
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_Message);
DB_WW_TL_OverlappingArmors(_Message,(CHARACTER)_Character,_Armor,(CHARACTER)_OtherCharacter,(ITEM)_StatsSource);
OpenMessageBoxYesNo(_Character,_Message);

// Or, if someone equips normal armor from which someone is already wearing a transmogrification using its stats that is restricted to one use at a time
IF
Equipped(_Armor,_Character)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationStatsSources(_Armor,_Transmogrification)
AND
GetTemplate(_Armor,_StatsTemplate)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_StatsTemplate,_,_,_,_,_,1,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrifications(_OtherCharacter,(ITEM)_Transmogrification)
AND
_Character != _OtherCharacter
AND
QRY_WW_TL_FetchString("WW_TL_ItemAlreadyInUseMessage")
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_Message)
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_Message);
DB_WW_TL_OverlappingArmors(_Message,(CHARACTER)_Character,_Armor,_OtherCharacter,_Transmogrification);
OpenMessageBoxYesNo(_Character,_Message);

// Changed in v1.0.3.15 to work with equipping items on other characters in party inventory screen (in which case message box won't be opened for character who equipped something)
IF
MessageBoxYesNoClosed(_,_Message,_Result)
AND
DB_WW_TL_OverlappingArmors(_Message,_Character,_,_,_)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_ResolveArmorConflict((GUIDSTRING)_Character,_Result);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_ResolveArmorConflict((GUIDSTRING)_Character,0)
AND
DB_WW_TL_OverlappingArmors(_Message,(CHARACTER)_Character,_Armor,_OtherCharacter,_OtherArmor)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_OverlappingArmors(_Message,_Character,_Armor,_OtherCharacter,_OtherArmor);
Unequip(_Character,_Armor);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_ResolveArmorConflict((GUIDSTRING)_Character,1)
AND
DB_WW_TL_OverlappingArmors(_Message,(CHARACTER)_Character,_Armor,_OtherCharacter,_OtherArmor)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_OverlappingArmors(_Message,_Character,_Armor,_OtherCharacter,_OtherArmor);
Unequip((CHARACTER)_OtherCharacter,_OtherArmor);

//END_REGION

//REGION Apply or remove a transmogrification's effects to the owner

//
// Removing boost
//

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_RemoveStatsBoost((GUIDSTRING)_Character,(STRING)_StatsBoost,(GUIDSTRING)_Armor)
AND
_StatsBoost != "NULL"
AND
NOT QRY_WW_TL_HasOtherStatusSource((CHARACTER)_Character,_StatsBoost,_Armor)
THEN
RemoveStatus(_Character,_StatsBoost);

QRY
QRY_WW_TL_HasOtherStatusSource((CHARACTER)_Character,(STRING)_StatsBoost,(GUIDSTRING)_ExcludedSource)
AND
DB_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrifications(_Character,_Source)
AND
_Source != _ExcludedSource
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Source,_,_,_StatsBoost)
THEN
DB_NOOP(1);

//
// Adding boost
//

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_ApplyStatsBoost((CHARACTER)_Character,(STRING)_StatsBoost)
AND
_StatsBoost != "NULL"
AND
HasActiveStatus((GUIDSTRING)_Character,_StatsBoost,0)
THEN
ApplyStatus(_Character,_StatsBoost,-1.0);

//
// Setting armor heaviness behaviors
//

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_ApplyArmorHeaviness((GUIDSTRING)_Character)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness(_Character)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_SetArmorLimitation((GUIDSTRING)_Character); // sets status for wearing transmogrified armor (light or heavier) in any slot
PROC_WW_TL_AdjustToBodyHeaviness((GUIDSTRING)_Character); // sets status for transmogrified armor type in chest/body slot

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_SetArmorLimitation((GUIDSTRING)_Character)
AND
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness_Overall(_Character,_)
AND
HasActiveStatus(_Character,"WW_TL_WEARING_ARMOR",1)
THEN
RemoveStatus(_Character,"WW_TL_WEARING_ARMOR");

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_SetArmorLimitation((GUIDSTRING)_Character)
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness_Overall(_Character,_Heaviness)
AND
_Heaviness == 0
AND
HasActiveStatus(_Character,"WW_TL_WEARING_ARMOR",1)
THEN
RemoveStatus(_Character,"WW_TL_WEARING_ARMOR");

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_SetArmorLimitation((GUIDSTRING)_Character)
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness_Overall(_Character,_Heaviness)
AND
_Heaviness > 0
AND
HasActiveStatus(_Character,"WW_TL_WEARING_ARMOR",0)
THEN
ApplyStatus(_Character,"WW_TL_WEARING_ARMOR",-1.0);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_SetArmorLimitation((GUIDSTRING)_Character)
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness_Overall(_Character,_Heaviness)
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness_Overall(_Character,_Heaviness);

// If there's not a transmogrified item equipped on the chest, get rid of the armor heaviness status
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_AdjustToBodyHeaviness((GUIDSTRING)_Character)
AND
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness_Chest(_Character,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorTypes(_,_Stat,_)
AND
HasActiveStatus(_Character,_Stat,1)
THEN
RemoveStatus(_Character,_Stat);

// Otherwise, change it to whatever the new heaviest level is if it's different
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_AdjustToBodyHeaviness((GUIDSTRING)_Character)
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness_Chest(_Character,_Heaviness)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorTypes(_Heaviness,_Stat,_)
AND
HasActiveStatus(_Character,_Stat,0)
THEN
ApplyStatus(_Character,_Stat,-1.0);

// cleanup the query return DB if the previous rule didn't meet all the requirements to execute
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_AdjustToBodyHeaviness((GUIDSTRING)_Character,(INTEGER)_HeavinessDelta)
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness_Chest(_Character,_Heaviness)
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_GetEquipmentHeaviness_Chest(_Character,_Heaviness);

//END_REGION

//REGION Impose restrictions on spell casts / cooldowns

// Record spells that need to cool down
IF
CastSpell(_Caster,_Spell,_,_,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorSpells(_Status,_Spell,_Cooldown)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_MeetsConditionsForCooldown(_Caster,_Cooldown) // added in v1.0.3.15
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_ItemTemplate,_,_Status,_,_,_,_,_)
//DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmor(_,_,_ItemTemplate,_Status) doesn't work if there is not currently transmogrifications with these stats
AND
QRY_WW_TL_UsingStatsFromTemplate((CHARACTER)_Caster,_ItemTemplate)
THEN
DB_WW_TL_CoolingDown(_ItemTemplate,_Spell,_Cooldown);

// If a spell is cast from a container that has a cooldown, record every spell within that container
IF
CastSpell(_Caster,_Spell,_,_,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorSpellContainers(_Container,_Spell)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorSpells(_Status,_Container,_Cooldown)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_MeetsConditionsForCooldown(_Caster,_Cooldown) // added in v1.0.3.15
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorComponents(_ItemTemplate,_,_Status,_,_,_,_,_)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_UsingStatsFromTemplate((CHARACTER)_Caster,_ItemTemplate)
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorSpellContainers(_Container,_EverySpell)
THEN
DB_WW_TL_CoolingDown(_ItemTemplate,_EverySpell,_Cooldown);

// added in v1.0.3.15
QRY
QRY_WW_TL_MeetsConditionsForCooldown((GUIDSTRING)_Character,_Cooldown)
AND
_Cooldown != "TURN"
AND
_Cooldown != "COMBAT"
THEN
DB_NOOP(1);

// added in v1.0.3.15
QRY
QRY_WW_TL_MeetsConditionsForCooldown((GUIDSTRING)_Character,"TURN")
AND
DB_Is_InCombat(_Character,_)
THEN
DB_NOOP(1);

// added in v1.0.3.15
QRY
QRY_WW_TL_MeetsConditionsForCooldown((GUIDSTRING)_Character,"COMBAT")
AND
DB_Is_InCombat(_Character,_)
THEN
DB_NOOP(1);

// Prevent casting spells from items that are still cooling down
// Changed in v1.0.5.3 to only apply to transmogrifications and not the original items
IF
StartedPreviewingSpell(_Caster,_Spell,_,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_CoolingDown(_ItemTemplate,_Spell,_)
AND
//QRY_WW_TL_UsingStatsFromTemplate((CHARACTER)_Caster,_ItemTemplate)
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Item,_,_ItemTemplate,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrifications(_Caster,(ITEM)_Item)
AND
GetPosition(_Caster,_X,_Y,_Z)
AND
RealSum(_X,0.01,_NewX)
THEN
CharacterMoveToPosition((CHARACTER)_Caster,_NewX,_Y,_Z,"Walk","");
RealtimeObjectTimerLaunch(_Caster,"WW_TL_InvalidCast_DelayMessage",500);

IF
ObjectTimerFinished(_Character,"WW_TL_InvalidCast_DelayMessage")
AND
QRY_WW_TL_FetchString("WW_TL_ItemSpellCooldownMessage")
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_Message)
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_Message);
OpenMessageBox((CHARACTER)_Character,_Message);

IF
TurnEnded(_Character)
AND
DB_WW_TL_CoolingDown(_ItemTemplate,_Spell,"TURN")
AND
QRY_WW_TL_UsingStatsFromTemplate((CHARACTER)_Character,_ItemTemplate)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_CoolingDown(_ItemTemplate,_Spell,"TURN");

// Added in v1.0.3.15
IF
CombatEnded(_CombatGuid)
AND
DB_WW_TL_CoolingDown(_ItemTemplate,_Spell,"TURN")
AND
DB_Was_InCombat(_Character,_CombatGuid)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_UsingStatsFromTemplate((CHARACTER)_Character,_ItemTemplate)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_CoolingDown(_ItemTemplate,_Spell,"TURN");

IF
CombatEnded(_CombatGuid)
AND
DB_WW_TL_CoolingDown(_ItemTemplate,_Spell,"COMBAT")
AND
DB_Was_InCombat(_Character,_CombatGuid)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_UsingStatsFromTemplate((CHARACTER)_Character,_ItemTemplate)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_CoolingDown(_ItemTemplate,_Spell,"COMBAT");

IF
ShortRested(_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_CoolingDown(_ItemTemplate,_Spell,"SHORT")
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_CoolingDown(_ItemTemplate,_Spell,"SHORT");

// Reset everything except Long Rest cooldowns when using a Potion of Angelic Reprieve
// Added in v1.0.4.0
IF
StatusApplied(_Character,"ALCH_POTION_REST_SLEEP_LESSER_RESTORATION",_,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_CoolingDown(_ItemTemplate,_Spell,_Cooldown)
AND
_Cooldown != "LONG"
AND
QRY_WW_TL_UsingStatsFromTemplate((CHARACTER)_Character,_ItemTemplate)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_CoolingDown(_ItemTemplate,_Spell,_Cooldown);

IF
LongRestFinished()
AND
DB_WW_TL_CoolingDown(_ItemTemplate,_Spell,_Cooldown)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_CoolingDown(_ItemTemplate,_Spell,_Cooldown);

// Reset all cooldowns when using a Potion of Angelic Slumber
// Added in v1.0.4.0
IF
StatusApplied(_Character,"ALCH_POTION_REST_SLEEP_GREATER_RESTORATION",_,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_CoolingDown(_ItemTemplate,_Spell,_Cooldown)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_UsingStatsFromTemplate((CHARACTER)_Character,_ItemTemplate)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_CoolingDown(_ItemTemplate,_Spell,_Cooldown);

// Reset all cooldowns when using a Restoration Pod, the pool in the House of Hope, etc.
// Added in v1.0.4.0
IF
StatusApplied(_Character,"TUT_RESTORATION",_,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_CoolingDown(_ItemTemplate,_Spell,_Cooldown)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_UsingStatsFromTemplate((CHARACTER)_Character,_ItemTemplate)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_CoolingDown(_ItemTemplate,_Spell,_Cooldown);

// Reset all cooldowns when anyone uses Divine Intervention to heal / rest
// Added in v1.0.4.0
IF
CastedSpell(_,"Shout_DivineIntervention_Healing",_,_,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_CoolingDown(_ItemTemplate,_Spell,_Cooldown)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_CoolingDown(_ItemTemplate,_Spell,_Cooldown);

//END_REGION

//REGION Impose restrictions on simple systems like unarmored statuses

// Changed in v1.0.3.15 to only be evaluated once per time interval to hopefully improve performance when lots of statuses are applied close together
IF
StatusApplied(_Character,"WW_TL_REMOVE_UNARMORED_STATUSES",_,_)
AND
ObjectTimerExists(_Character,"WW_TL_RemoveUnarmoredStatusesDelay",0)
THEN
RealtimeObjectTimerLaunch(_Character,"WW_TL_RemoveUnarmoredStatusesDelay",500);

// Changed in v1.0.3.15
IF
ObjectTimerFinished(_Character,"WW_TL_RemoveUnarmoredStatusesDelay")
THEN
RemoveStatus(_Character,"WW_TL_REMOVE_UNARMORED_STATUSES");

// Changed in v1.0.3.15
IF
ObjectTimerFinished(_Character,"WW_TL_RemoveUnarmoredStatusesDelay")
AND
DB_WW_TL_UnarmoredOnlyStatuses(_InvalidStatus)
AND
HasActiveStatus(_Character,_InvalidStatus,1)
THEN
RemoveStatus(_Character,_InvalidStatus);

// Prevent characters from raging while they're wearing heavy armor b/c the debuffs won't be applied like they should
IF
StatusApplied(_Barbarian,_Rage,_,_)
AND
IsStatusFromGroup(_Rage,"SG_Rage",1)
AND
HasActiveStatus(_Barbarian,"WW_TL_ARMOR_BEHAVIOUR_HEAVY",1)
THEN
RemoveStatus(_Barbarian,_Rage); // not using Shout_EndRage b/c shouldn't have been able to rage at all, so don't want to allow bonuses applied at end of rage
UseSpell(_Barbarian,"Shout_WW_TL_RefundRage",_Barbarian);

// Open a message box to warn the player that their rage was refunded
// Added in v1.0.6.31
IF
CastedSpell(_Barbarian,"Shout_WW_TL_RefundRage",_,_,_)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_FetchString("WW_TL_RageRefundMessage")
AND
DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_Warning)
THEN
NOT DB_QRYRTN_WW_TL_FetchString(_Warning);
OpenMessageBox((CHARACTER)_Barbarian,_Warning);

IF
StatusApplied(_Barbarian,"WW_TL_ARMOR_BEHAVIOUR_HEAVY",_,_)
AND
HasActiveStatusWithGroup(_Barbarian,"SG_Rage",1)
THEN
UseSpell(_Barbarian,"Shout_EndRage",_Barbarian);

//END_REGION

//REGION Remove armour boosts during Wild Shape and other polymorph statuses

// Toggle transmogrified equipment back on when character loses polymorph / Wild Shape
// Added in v1.0.3.0
IF
ShapeshiftChanged(_Character,_,_,_Status)
AND
DB_WW_TL_DisabledBonuses((CHARACTER)_Character)
AND
NOT QRY_WW_TL_StatusDisablesEquipment(_Status)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_DisabledBonuses(_Character);
PROC_WW_TL_ReapplyDisabledBonuses(_Character);

// Added in v1.0.3.0
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_ReapplyDisabledBonuses((CHARACTER)_Character)
AND
DB_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrifications((GUIDSTRING)_Character,_Item)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors((GUIDSTRING)_Item,_,_,_StatsBoost)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_ApplyStatsBoost((CHARACTER)_Character,_StatsBoost);

// Added in v1.0.3.0
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_ReapplyDisabledBonuses((CHARACTER)_Character)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_ApplyArmorHeaviness(_Character);

// Toggle transmogrified equipment off when owner polymorphs / Wild Shapes
// NOTE: _Status is the current shapeshift status (can be stacked on top of others), or "" if no shapeshift status (e.g. just lost the last one)
// Added in v1.0.3.0
IF
ShapeshiftChanged(_Character,_,_,_Status)
AND
NOT DB_WW_TL_DisabledBonuses((CHARACTER)_Character)
AND
QRY_WW_TL_StatusDisablesEquipment(_Status)
AND
DB_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrifications((GUIDSTRING)_Character,_)
THEN
DB_WW_TL_DisabledBonuses((CHARACTER)_Character);

// Wait a short time in case any armour boosts/passives need to do something in response to the status being applied
// Added in v1.0.3.0
IF
DB_WW_TL_DisabledBonuses(_Character)
THEN
RealtimeObjectTimerLaunch(_Character,"WW_TL_DisablingBonusesDelay",10);

// Remove every individual equipment boost
// Added in v1.0.3.0
IF
ObjectTimerFinished(_Character,"WW_TL_DisablingBonusesDelay")
AND
DB_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrifications(_Character,_Item)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors((GUIDSTRING)_Item,_,_,_StatsBoost)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_RemoveStatsBoost(_Character,_StatsBoost,_Item);

// Remove the current armor type behavior (if any)
// Added in v1.0.3.0
IF
ObjectTimerFinished(_Character,"WW_TL_DisablingBonusesDelay")
AND
DB_WW_TL_ArmorTypes(_,_Stat,_)
AND
HasActiveStatus(_Character,_Stat,1)
THEN
RemoveStatus(_Character,_Stat);

// Remove the status for wearing transmogrified armour
// Added in v1.0.3.15
IF
ObjectTimerFinished(_Character,"WW_TL_DisablingBonusesDelay")
AND
HasActiveStatus(_Character,"WW_TL_WEARING_ARMOR",1)
THEN
RemoveStatus(_Character,"WW_TL_WEARING_ARMOR");

// Added in v1.0.3.0
QRY
QRY_WW_TL_StatusDisablesEquipment((STRING)_Status)
AND
IsStatusFromGroup(_Status,"SG_Polymorph",1)
AND
IsStatusFromGroup(_Status,"SG_Disguise",0)
AND
NOT DB_WW_TL_PolymorphStatusExceptionsForAC(_Status)
THEN
DB_NOOP(1);

// Added in v1.0.3.0
QRY
QRY_WW_TL_StatusDisablesEquipment((STRING)_Status)
AND
IsStatusFromGroup(_Status,"SG_Polymorph_BeastShape",1)
AND
NOT DB_WW_TL_PolymorphStatusExceptionsForAC(_Status)
THEN
DB_NOOP(1);

//END_REGION

//REGION Trigger reevaluations of AC after statuses are removed

// Wait a short time after a status is removed to trigger an AC reevaluation for characters wearing transmogged equipment
// (prevents unnecessary work if lots of statuses are added and removed very rapidly by only checking once after it's done)
// Added in v1.0.3.0
IF
StatusRemoved(_Character,_Status,_,_)
AND
NOT DB_WW_TL_StatusesIgnoredForAC(_Status) // changed in v1.0.6.31
AND
DB_WW_TL_EquippedTransmogrifications(_Character,_)
AND
ObjectTimerExists(_Character,"WW_TL_TriggerACReevaluation",0)
THEN
RealtimeObjectTimerLaunch(_Character,"WW_TL_TriggerACReevaluation",500);

// Added in v1.0.3.0
IF
ObjectTimerFinished(_Character,"WW_TL_TriggerACReevaluation")
THEN
ApplyStatus(_Character,"WW_TL_REEVALUATE_OVERRIDES",0.2,1,_Character);

//END_REGION

//REGION Spell to destroy transmogrifications

IF
UsingSpellOnTarget(_Caster,_Target,"Target_WW_TL_Transmogrify_Destroy",_,_,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Target,_,_,_)
THEN
DB_WW_TL_DestroyingTransmogrifications(_Target);

IF
CastSpell(_Caster,"Target_WW_TL_Transmogrify_Destroy",_,_,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_DestroyingTransmogrifications(_Target)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_DestroyingTransmogrifications(_Target);
PROC_WW_TL_SpellDestroyEffect((ITEM)_Target);
PROC_WW_TL_DestroyTransmogrification(_Target,_Caster);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_SpellDestroyEffect((ITEM)_Item)
AND
GetRegion((GUIDSTRING)_Item,_Region)
AND
DB_CurrentLevel(_Region)
AND
GetPosition(_Item,_X,_Y,_Z)
THEN
PlayEffectAtPosition((EFFECTRESOURCE)VFX_Script_Stub_Poof_01_f0cf792a-0f74-d17e-ad0d-6052a6131416,_X,_Y,_Z);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_DestroyTransmogrification((ITEM)_Item,(GUIDSTRING)_Caster)
AND
IsTagged((GUIDSTRING)_Item,(TAG)WW_TL_TRANSMOGRIFIED_f4a99f6c-34e9-4958-9ce7-3b4d01e61e07,1)
THEN
PROC_WW_TL_WipeTransmogrificationDBs(_Item,_Caster);
RequestDelete((ITEM)_Item);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_WipeTransmogrificationDBs((GUIDSTRING)_Item,(GUIDSTRING)_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationStatsSources(_Source,_Item)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationStatsSources(_Source,_Item);

PROC
PROC_WW_TL_WipeTransmogrificationDBs((GUIDSTRING)_Item,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Item,_Heaviness,_OriginalTemplate,_StatsBoost)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_TransmogrifiedArmors(_Item,_Heaviness,_OriginalTemplate,_StatsBoost);

// Added in v1.0.1
PROC
PROC_WW_TL_WipeTransmogrificationDBs((GUIDSTRING)_Item,_)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationDisplayNames(_Item,_DisplayName)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationDisplayNames(_Item,_DisplayName);

// Disabled in v1.0.5.3
//PROC
//PROC_WW_TL_WipeTransmogrificationDBs((GUIDSTRING)_Item,(GUIDSTRING)_Caster)
//AND
//DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationDyes((ITEM)_Item,_DyeTemplate)
//THEN
//NOT DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationDyes(_Item,_DyeTemplate);
//PROC_WW_TL_RefundDyeToCharacter(_DyeTemplate,_Caster);

//END_REGION

// Reapply boosts to characters that are resurrected
// Removed in v1.0.4 because added ApplyToDead flag to armor statuses instead
//IF
//StatusApplied(_Character,"RESURRECTING",_,_)
//THEN
//PROC_WW_TL_ReapplyDisabledBonuses((CHARACTER)_Character);

// Must occur after region that handles items being unequipped or else the item will be deleted before its status boost is removed
IF
Unequipped(_Item,_Character)
AND
DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationBeingOverwritten(_Item)
THEN
NOT DB_WW_TL_TransmogrificationBeingOverwritten(_Item);
PROC_WW_TL_DestroyTransmogrification(_Item,_Character);
